Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a nonequilibrium algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 51. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 218/3.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to nonequilibrium theory, we decompose the problem into 51 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 218. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.

Theorem 212.6 (Bound Derivation)
